```
def inverse_h_update(a, lam, gamma, eps):
   h = correlate_lambda_a(lam,a)
   h = max(h, eps)
   target = np.dot(a,h) # weighted mean? Or np.mean(h)?
   ratio = target / h
   a_new = a * (ratio ** gamma)
   normalize sum=1
```

Pseudo local optimizer:
```
def local_search_peel(a, budget, seed):
    a = normalize_sum1(a)
    best=a; best_score=score(a)
    beta=maybe 10/scale; gamma=0.2
    momentum? inverse update with noise
    for loop until time:
       c=convolve(a)
       M=max(c)
       score=2n*M
       update best
       # choose λ
       # To avoid overflow: z = β(c - M) / M? c positive.
       # Maybe use p-power: λ = max(0, c/M - τ)^p / sum
       # Use adaptive τ/β. Start τ=0.8? We want all peaks.
       # maybe combine: u = np.exp(β*(c-M)/M); λ = u/sum. For β=0.1 broad, β=10 sharp.
       h = correlate_lambda_a(λ,a)
       h_safe = np.maximum(h, 1e-15)
       target = exp(weighted log h_safe)
       ratio = target/h_safe
       # clip ratio^gamma to avoid blow up
       ratio = np.clip(ratio, 0.1, 10)
       a_new = a * (ratio ** gamma)
       # add multiplicative noise for exploration? a_new *= exp(eps normal) maybe rarely
       a_new = normalize_sum1(a_new)
       # optionally blend with best if worse?
       if score_new <= current*1.01 or random: accept
       else reject and reduce gamma
       if stagnation: restart with smooth perturbation of best or change beta/gamma
```

```
c = conv(a)
M = max(c)
score = 2nM
pow_param = p (e.g., 3)
w = c**(p-1)  # if p close 1, all 1

hclip = max(h, eps)
# weighted mean to preserve sum:
gm = exp(average(log hclip, weights=a))
ratio = gm/hclip
a_new = a * ratio**eta
a_new = normalize(a_new)

Local search design detailed:
```
def local_search(a, budget, seed, label):
    n=a.size; t0; deadline
    a=normalize_sum1(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score(a)
    # parameters
    rho_start=1.0? rho_end=20? 
    eta=0.05? step size for log update ratio^eta
    noise=0.01
    p_cycle: rho = [1,2,4,8,16,32,64]? 
    # maintain current accept score
    rng...
    it=0; stagnant=0
    current=a; current_score=best_score
    while time<deadline:
       it++
       c=fast_convolve(current)
       M=max(c); current_score=2nM
       if current_score<best_score: best=...
       # choose rho based on maybe stagnation and it
       # e.g., rho_cycle[i % len], or adaptive: if M/avg >1.2 use high, else low
       avg=1/(2n-1); ratio=M/avg
       # low ratio -> optimize flatness, rho=2-4; high -> rho=10-30
       rho = min(64, max(1, 4*math.log(max(ratio,1.0))))? 
       # But need annealing.
       w = pow_weights(c, rho, rng?)
       h=correlate(w,current)
       h=max(h, 1e-18)
       logtarget = weighted log h using weights=current
       ratio = exp(logtarget - logh)
       # limit
       ratio = np.clip(ratio, 0.5, 2.0)
       cand = current * (ratio**eta)
       if noise>0: cand *= exp(normal(0,noise,n)); normalize
       c2=conv(cand); cand_score=...
       # accept if cand_score <= current_score*(1+accept_rel) or random
       if cand_score < best_score: best...
       if accept: current=cand; stagnant=0? if improvement reset
       else: reduce eta; stagnant++
       if stagnant>threshold: restart from best with perturbation or change rho; if too many break
       # occasionally evaluate top? no
    return best,best_score
```

Now let's test mathematical update with L_p gradient maybe more standard. Instead of inverse-h, maybe use projected gradient on F=∑c^p. We can implement gradient `g = correlate(w,a)` where w=c^{p-1}/sum. Then log update: `a *= exp(-eta*(g - weighted_mean_g))`. This is simpler and probably more effective than inverse-h? Which is better? Let's compare:
- Gradient descent on F: reduces high c directly; if h_i high, reduce a_i. `a_i ∝ exp(-eta h_i)`. Inverse-h: `a_i ∝ exp(-eta log h_i)` (since logtarget - logh = -log h + const). The log damps reduction less for small h and more for large? Actually if h_i=10, gradient update factor exp(-eta*10), inverse exp(-eta*log10). For large h, gradient much stronger; for small h=0.1, gradient factor exp(eta*0.1) small increase, inverse exp(+eta*log10) increase exp(eta*2.3). Inverse may over-increase small h. Gradient likely more stable. But inverse h directly promotes constant h, which is KKT. In KKT we want h_i constant, not proportional to exp(-h). Exp(-h) drives h away? If h_i high, reduce; if low, increase; but gradient uses linear h, which is standard for F. For objective F, gradient of log-a update with h (not log h) is correct. Let's use gradient descent on F, not inverse-h. We can still target KKT; the stationary of F in log parameters is a_i*(h_i - weighted mean)=0, i.e., h_i constant for positive a. So log update with exp(-eta h) converges to same KKT as inverse-h asymptotically (since for small h, log h ~ h?) but with proper metric. Good.

Thus algorithm:
```

h = correlate(w,a)  # since w sum1; derivative of ∑ w_k c_k = 2h_i. Also derivative of F = 2∑ (p c^{p-1}) a = constant * h if w normalized? Yes.
h_center = h - weighted_mean(h,a) # or h - mean? For log update, to preserve sum first order, subtract weighted mean ∑ a_i h_i / S.
step = eta * h_center / scale? h values small ~1/n? If sum=1, a mean 1/n, h ~ average a ~1/n. For n=4096, h~2.4e-4. If eta=1, step ~1e-4 tiny. Need scale by 2n? Scale gradient to O(1). ```
Then `a_new = a * exp(-eta * g)`. Normalize. This is multiplicative gradient descent on F with natural gradient? Let's derive: L =∑ w_k c_k; gradient w.r.t a_i=2h_i. def local_search_gradient(a,budget,seed):
   normalize sum1
   best...

   for cycle rho in [1,2,3,5,8,14,24,40,64,100]? 
      for inner iterations or time:
         c=conv(a); M; score
         update best
         if stagnation or time block: change rho
         w=pow_weights(c,rho)
         h=correlate(w,a)
         # weighted mean: mu=dot(a,h) (S=1)
         g = 2*n*(h - mu)  # O(1)
         # normalize gradient magnitude? g max maybe few. Could scale by maxabs to robust:
         scale=max(abs(g))
         if scale<1e-12: break
         g=g/scale

         normalize
         score_new; accept if <= current*(1+accept_rel) or good; else reduce eta
```

def normalize_sum1(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    # floor? maybe min positive = 1e-12/n? To avoid zeros.
    n=a.size
    eps = 1e-12 / max(1,n)
    a += eps? This changes sum slightly. We can set floor=1e-10/n? For n=8192, floor 1e-14, sum 8e-11 negligible. Use 1e-12 maybe too high if n=1e5 (1e-7). We won't. 
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-14)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: a=np.full(n,1/n)
    else a/=s
    # if max>1? after sum=1 max≤1.
    return a
```

def active_basis_centered(a, c, idx):
    n=a.size; K=len(idx)
    B = np.empty((n,K), dtype=np.float64)
    if K==0: return B, None
    for t,k in enumerate(idx):
        k=int(k)
        start=max(0, k-(n-1)); stop=min(n, k+1); # indices i such that 0<=k-i<n => i∈[k-n+1, k]
        # b_i = a_{k-i}. For i=start..stop-1, source index k-i. When i=start, src=k-start <= n-1; when i=stop-1, src=k-(stop-1) >=0.
        src_start = k - (stop-1); src_end = k - start +1
        b = np.zeros(n)
        b[start:stop] = a[src_start:src_end][::-1]
        mu = float(np.dot(a,b)) # should be c[k]

    return B
```

λ solve details:
```
def solve_simplex_lambda(M, K, iters=12):
   lam = np.full(K, 1.0/K)
   # Add diag regularization
   diag = np.diag(M).copy()
   maxdiag = max(np.max(diag),1e-300)
   lam = lam.copy()
   for _ in range(iters):
       grad = M @ lam
       # If grad all same? break
       gscale = np.max(np.abs(grad))
       if gscale < 1e-30:
          break
       # gradient direction; use step 0.5/gscale? Multiplicative exp(-eta*grad/gscale)
       eta_lam = 0.5
       # To avoid all mass on one, clip
       adj = grad / gscale
       # Center? constant invariant; but centering reduces dynamic range
       # since lam sum1, use adj -= dot(lam,adj)
       adj -= float(np.dot(lam, adj))
       factor = np.exp(eta_lam * adj) # because minimize λ^T Mλ, gradient 2Mλ; reduce high grad: λ*=exp(-eta grad). If adj centered, factor >1 for low, <1 high. Wait minimize: λ_k ← λ_k * exp(-η grad_k). If adj=grad/gscale centered, low adj negative -> exp(positive) increase, high positive decrease. Use factor=exp(-eta*adj). But if centered, subtract dot? constant; sign. Let's do `factor = exp(-eta_lam*adj)`; normalize.
       lam = lam * factor
       # floor
       lam = 0.999*lam + 1e-12
       lam /= sum
   return lam
```
Check sign: Objective q=λ M λ. Gradient = 2(Mλ - maybe symmetric). For positive M, if grad_k high, want lower λ. factor exp(-η grad) -> high grad decreases. Good. Centering: if adj = grad - weighted mean, constant removed; sign same. Use `factor=np.exp(-0.7*adj)`. If adj range [-1,1], factor [0.49,2.02]. Good.
- Could alternatively use `lam = project(lam - step*grad, simplex)` but projection O(K log K). Multiplicative fine.
- Need M symmetric; due to numeric, symmetrize `M=(M+M.T)*0.5`.

Active selection and K schedule:

def get_active_indices(c, K):
   L=c.size
   if K>=L: return np.arange(L)
   # top K
   top = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
   # optionally include 10% of max if count not too large? If top arbitrary, any top K enough.
   return top
```
Could sort? Not needed.

Now local_search_active pseudo with phases:
```
def local_search_active(a, budget, seed, maxK):
   a=normalize_sum1(a); n
   best=a; best_score=fast_score(a)
   current=a; current_score=best_score
   eta=0.2; noise=0.01; stagnant=0; it=0; t0

       if gscale < 1e-14: stagnant large; break

       # Add small noise to log update? Could after candidate.

          eta=0.3; noise=0.02; stagnant=0
       # optional K adapt: every 1000 it alt K?
   return best,best_score

Restart perturbation:
```
def smooth_perturb(a, rng, strength):
   n=a.size
   t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   z=0
   for m in 1..6: amp=strength/(m+1); ph=rng.uniform; z += amp*np.cos(2πm t+ph); also sine
   a = a * exp(z)
   # optionally local bump
   return normalize_sum1(a)
```

```
def active_basis(a, c, idx):
    n=a.size; K=len(idx); B=np.zeros((n,K), dtype=np.float64)
    # maybe if K==0 return
    for j,k in enumerate(idx):
       k=int(k)
       start = 0 if k <= n-1 else k-(n-1)
       stop = n if k >= n-1 else k+1
       # if stop>start
       src_start = k - (stop-1)
       src_end = k - start + 1
       B[start:stop,j] = a[src_start:src_end][::-1]
       B[:,j] -= float(c[k])
    return B
```

For k=L-1=2n-2: start=n-1,stop=n, src_start=0,src_end=1; B[n-1]=a[0]; c_last=a0^2? Actually c_{2n-2}=a_{n-1}^2? Wait convolution index max shift: c_{2n-2}=a_{n-1}a_{n-1}. M regularization: 
```
def solve_simplex_lambda(M,K):
    lam=np.full(K,1.0/K)
    M=(M+M.T)*0.5
    # if K==1 return [1]
    # scale M to reduce overflow: maxabs = max(abs(M)). If maxabs>0, M=M/maxabs, lambda unaffected? Objective scaled, same minimizer. Use M_norm.
    maxabs = float(np.max(np.abs(M))) if K>0 else 1.0
    if maxabs > 1e-300:
       M = M / maxabs
    # regularization relative
    reg = 1e-10 * max(float(np.max(np.abs(np.diag(M)))),1e-10)
    diag = np.diag_indices(K)
    M[diag] += reg
    # multiplicative iterations
    for _ in range(16):
       grad = M @ lam
       gmin=grad.min(); gmax=grad.max()
       if not finite: break
       center = float(np.dot(lam,grad))
       adj = grad - center
       rng? no
       r = adj.max()-adj.min()
       if r < 1e-14: break
       # scale so max abs adj ~1
       scale = max(abs(adj.max()), abs(adj.min()))
       if scale < 1e-14: break
       adj /= scale
       factor = np.exp(-0.7*adj)
       lam = lam * factor
       # floor to prevent zeros
       lam = lam + 1e-12
       lam = 0.9999*lam + 1e-12/K? Simpler:
       minlam=1e-12/K
       lam = np.maximum(lam, minlam)
       lam /= lam.sum()
    return lam
```

New grad scaled? iter: lam .8,.2, grad=[.8,.4], center=.76, adj=[.04? .8-.76=.04, .4-.76=-.36]; factor=[exp(-0.077), exp(0.972)]? Wait sign: We use factor exp(-0.7*adj). For high d and low λ? In this example d2=2 high, current lam2 low=.2; grad2=.4 < center? adj negative -> factor >1, increases lam2. Good. Converges to lam∝1/d=[2/3,1/3]. Good. Sign correct.

def sanitize_for_return(seq):
   a=np.asarray(seq,float)
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<0.01: a=np.ones(1024); return [1.0]*1024
   # scale to sum=1? If max*scale <=1000. Since s maybe not 1. scale=1/s? Better keep sum around 1 to avoid small max? If scale=1.0/s, sum=1. If max/s? max after scale = max/s. If max/s >1000, set max=1000 and renormalize? But if sum huge? Our search sum1; max≤1. scale safe. Use target sum = 1.0. If s>0, a/=s. But if max after scale >1000 (only if s<max/1000; impossible if s≥max for nonneg? max/s≤1), safe.
   a = a / s
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   if a.sum()<0.01: fallback
   return [float(x) for x in a]
```

However gradient update multiplicative acts relative; if small coord is important, h there may be large relative? Weighted variance by a may ignore small a but its c could be high. The KKT gradient condition (for sum) requires weighted mean zero, but the choice λ to make h constant should perhaps minimize Euclidean variance of h, not a-weighted. If a_i small, its h can still cause product? Multiplicative update log a: gradient w.r.t x_i = a_i(g_i-avg). Stationarity g_i constant for all i positive, weighted by a in objective but condition still constant across positive i. But coordinates with larger a have more impact; weighted by sqrt(a) reduces small ones. Let's examine. The objective when updating log a: change in active loss L=∑λ c - constant*∑a. First-order change: ∑ a_i (g_i - μ) da? Parameter x: dL = ∑ a_i g_i d x_i. The norm of direction in x maybe; choosing λ to minimize ∑ a_i (g_i-μ)^2 aligns with Hessian in x? Since Hessian in log coordinates of active loss maybe ∑ a_i g_i^2? Not exactly. Weighted by a makes sense for natural metric. Good.

def local_search_active(a, budget_s, seed=0):
    if budget_s <= 0: return normalize_sum1(a), fast_score(...)?
    a=normalize_sum1(a)
    n=a.size
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget_s
    c=fast_convolve(a); best_score=2*n*float(c.max()); best=a.copy(); current=a.copy(); current_score=best_score
    base_K = ...
    K_cur=base_K; K_max=...
    eta=0.25; accept_rel=0.015; noise=0.01; stagnant=0; it=0
    mom=np.zeros(n)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    while time<deadline-0.05:
       if not finite current... reset
       c=fast_convolve(current)
       M=float(c.max()); sc=2*n*M
       if sc < best_score:
           best=current.copy(); best_score=sc; stagnant=0
           print? maybe not here to avoid spam; but can print every many.
       idx=select_active(c,K_cur)
       B=active_basis(current,c,idx)
       sqrt_a=np.sqrt(current)
       Bw=B*sqrt_a[:,None]
       Mmat=Bw.T@Bw
       Mmat=(Mmat+Mmat.T)*0.5
       lam=solve_simplex_lambda(Mmat,K_cur)
       g=B@lam
       g-=float(np.dot(current,g))
       # optional momentum
       mom = 0.5*mom + 0.5*g
       g=mom - dot(current,mom)? Wait mom already weighted mean zero? If g centered and a same, dot(current,mom)=0.5dot current g + old maybe current changed, not zero. Recenter: g -= dot(current,g)
       gscale=max(abs(g))
       if gscale < 1e-12: stagnant += 1; if >200 break; continue
       g /= gscale
       cand = current * np.exp(-eta*g)
       # occasional smooth noise? if it%100==0: cand *= 1+ small smooth? maybe skip.
       cand=normalize_sum1(cand)
       c2=fast_convolve(cand); sc2=2*n*float(c2.max())
       if finite:
          if sc2 <= current_score*(1+accept_rel):
             current=cand; current_score=sc2
             mom*=0.7
             if sc2 < best_score*(1-1e-10):
                 eta=min(1.0, eta*1.08)
                 accept_rel=max(0.0005, accept_rel*0.995)
             else:
                 eta=max(0.03, eta*0.93)
          else:
             eta=max(0.03, eta*0.75)
             mom*=0.5
             stagnant +=1
       else: eta... stagnant +=1
       if stagnant > 350:
           strength = min(0.25, 0.04 + 0.0002*stagnant)
           cand2=smooth_log_perturb(best,rng,strength)
           sc3=fast_score(cand2)
           if finite and sc3 < best_score*1.08:
              current=cand2; current.score=sc3
           else: current=best.copy(); current_score=best_score
           eta=0.3; mom=zeros; stagnant=0
           # adapt K
           if K_cur < K_max: K_cur = min(K_max, int(K_cur*1.5)+8)
           elif K_cur > K_min: K_cur = K_min
           accept_rel=0.03
       it +=1
    # Final: maybe return best
    return best,best_score
```

def smooth_log_perturb(a,rng,strength):
   n=a.size
   out=a.copy()
   if n>3 and strength>0:
       t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
       z=np.zeros(n)
       for m in range(1,6):
           amp=strength/(m*0.8+0.5)
           ph=rng.uniform(0,2π)
           z += amp*np.cos(2π m t + ph)
           ph2=rng.uniform...
           z += amp*np.sin(...)
       out *= np.exp(z)
       # optional local random bump
       if rng.random()<0.5:
          width=max(2,n//50); start=rng.integers(0,n-width); out[start:start+width]*=np.exp(rng.normal(0,strength,width))
   return normalize_sum1(out)
```

```
def random_wave_start(n, rng):
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    z=np.zeros(n)
    nf=int(rng.integers(3,12))
    for m in range(1,nf+1):
       amp=rng.uniform(0.3,2.0)/(m**0.6)
       ph=rng.uniform(0,2π)
       z += amp*np.cos(2π*m*t+ph)
       z += amp*rng.normal? no
    # random linear asym
    z += rng.uniform(-2,2)*(2*t-1)
    a=np.exp(z)
    return a
```

def two_block_start(n,rng):
   frac=float(rng.uniform(0.15,0.85)); m=int(frac*n); ratio=float(rng.uniform(0.05,8.0)); 
   # left high if ratio? Or random side
   base=1.0; high=ratio
   a=np.empty(n); if rng.random()<0.5: a[:m]=high; a[m:]=base else a[:m]=base; a[m:]=high
   return a

Gaussian clusters:
```
def cluster_start(n,rng):
   k=int(rng.integers(2,5)); t=np.linspace(0,1,n); a=np.full(n,0.01)
   for _ in range(k):
       pos=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.95)); w=float(rng.uniform(0.02,0.12)); amp=float(rng.expovariate(1.0)+0.2)
       a += amp*np.exp(-0.5*((t-pos)/w)**2)
   return a
```
Good.

def ushape_start(n,rng):

def run_level(n, budget, parent, seed):
   if budget <= 5:
      a=upsample(parent,n) if parent is not None else ones
      a=normalize_sum1(a); return a,fast_score(a)
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   starts=generate_starts(n,rng,parent, max_starts=100)
   starts.sort(key=_pair_score)
   # choose k
   if budget>600: ks=[0.5,0.25,0.15,0.1]
   elif budget>200: ks=[0.55,0.3,0.15]
   elif budget>80: ks=[0.65,0.35]
   else: ks=[1.0]
   k=min(len(starts),len(ks))
   best_seq,best_score = starts[0][1], starts[0][0]
   for i in range(k):
      time_this = ks[i]*budget (but update remaining?)
      seq,sc=local_search_active(starts[i][1], time_this, seed=seed+i)
      if sc<best_score: update
   return best_seq,best_score
```

```
def project_simplex(v):
    # minimize ||z-v||^2 s.t. z>=0, sum=1
    K=len(v)
    if K==0: return
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u*(np.arange(1,K+1)) - (cssv-1) > 0)[0]
    if len(rho)==0: return np.full(K,1/K)
    rho=rho[-1]
    theta=(cssv[rho]-1)/(rho+1)
    z=np.maximum(v-theta,0)
    s=z.sum(); if s<=0: z=full; else z/=s
    return z
```
This standard for simplex (nonnegative sum1). Good.

```
def solve_lambda(M,K):
   if K==1: return np.ones(1)
   M=(M+M.T)*0.5
   mmax=...
   if mmax>1e-300: M=M/mmax else M=identity
   reg=1e-10
   Mcopy=M.copy(); np.fill_diagonal(Mcopy, np.diag(Mcopy)+reg)
   lam=None
   try:
       x=np.linalg.solve(Mcopy, np.ones(K))
       if finite: lam=project_simplex(x)
   except: lam=None
   # refine with multiplicative
   if lam is None: lam=full(1/K)
   else: lam = 0.9*lam+0.1/K? maybe projection good; no.
   for _ in range(8):
       grad=M@lam
       center=dot(lam,grad); adj=grad-center
       scale=max(abs(adj.max()), abs(adj.min()))
       if scale<1e-13: break
       adj/=scale
       lam *= np.exp(-0.5*adj)
       lam += 1e-12; lam/=sum
   # ensure sum1
   return lam
```

def select_active(c,K):
    L=c.size; M=float(np.max(c))
    if K>=L or M<=0: return np.arange(L)
    # top K baseline
    top=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
    if K <= 1: return top
    # threshold = M * (1 - 1.0/(1.0 + 2.0*K))? We want include peaks close to max up to K. Simpler 0.95M.

def choose_K_values(n):
    if n <= 256: base=24; maxK=48
    elif n <= 512: base=32; maxK=64
    elif n <= 1024: base=48; maxK=96
    elif n <= 2048: base=80; maxK=160
    else: base=128; maxK=256
    # cap by L=2n-1
    maxK=min(maxK, 2*n-1)
    return base,maxK

```
"""..."""
import numpy as np
import time, math

def _pair_score(pair): return float(pair[0])

def normalize_sum1(a):
   arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
   n=arr.size
   if n==0: return np.array([1.0])
   arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
   arr=np.maximum(arr,0.0)
   arr=np.maximum(arr,1e-12)
   s=arr.sum()
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
       return np.full(n,1.0/n)
   arr/=s
   return arr
```
If arr all zeros, arr becomes floor 1e-12 each, s=n*1e-12, normalized to 1/n? Yes because all equal floor. Good.

`upsample_sequence`:
```
def upsample_sequence(seq,n):
   arr=np.asarray(seq,float)
   if arr.size==0 or n<=0: return np.ones((int(n),))
   if arr.size==n: return normalize_sum1(arr)
   # if n ==1 return normalize?
   x=np.linspace(0,1,arr.size); y=np.linspace(0,1,int(n))
   out=np.interp(y,x,arr)
   return normalize_sum1(out)
```
If seq length 1, x [0], y all 0? `np.linspace(0,1,1)` x=0; np.interp with x length1? It works? `np.interp(y, [0], arr)` returns arr[0] for y<0? For y>0, returns last? Actually np.interp requires xp length>=2 for linear; if len1, returns fp? It may not. Handle:
```

```

`next_power_two(x)`:
```
def next_power_two(x):
   if x<=1: return 1
   return 1 << (int(x)-1).bit_length()
```

`fast_convolve_fixed(a,N,L)`:
```
def fast_convolve_fixed(a,N,L):
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:L]
   c=np.ascontiguousarray(c)
   np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
   return c
```
`fast_convolve(a)`:
```
n=len(a); L=2*n-1; N=next_power_two(L); return fast_convolve_fixed(a,N,L)
```
`fast_score(a)`:
```
c=fast_convolve(a); n=a.size; return 2.0*float(n)*float(np.max(c))
```

       col=B[:,j]
       col.fill(0.0)
       start = 0 if k <= n-1 else k-(n-1)
       stop = n if k >= n-1 else k+1
       if stop>start:
           src_start = k - (stop - 1)
           src_end = k - start + 1
           col[start:stop] = a[src_start:src_end][::-1]
       col -= float(c[k])
   return K
```
Check for k valid 0..L-1. Good. 

`project_simplex(v)`: top-level.
```
def project_simplex(v):
   K=len(v)
   if K==0: return np.array([])
   if K==1: return np.ones(1)
   u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
   cssv=np.cumsum(u)
   rho=np.nonzero(u*(np.arange(1,K+1)) - (cssv-1) > 0)[0]
   if len(rho)==0:
       return np.full(K,1.0/K)
   rho=int(rho[-1])
   theta=(cssv[rho]-1.0)/(rho+1.0)
   z=v-theta
   z=np.maximum(z,0.0)
   s=float(z.sum())
   if s<=0: return np.full(K,1.0/K)
   return z/s
```

`solve_simplex_lambda(M,K)`:
```
def solve_simplex_lambda(M,K):
   if K==1: return np.ones(1)
   M=np.asarray(M)
   if M.size==0: ...
   M=(M+M.T)*0.5
   mmax=float(np.max(np.abs(M)))
   if not np.isfinite(mmax) or mmax < 1e-300:
       return np.full(K,1.0/K)
   M = M / mmax
   # regularize
   diag=np.diag_indices(K)
   M[diag] += 1e-10
   lam=None
   try:
       x=np.linalg.solve(M, np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64))
       if np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
           lam=project_simplex(x)
   except Exception:
       lam=None
   if lam is None:
       lam=np.full(K,1.0/K)
   # refine multiplicative
   for _ in range(8):
       grad=M.dot(lam)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): break
       center=float(np.dot(lam,grad))
       adj=grad-center
       amin=float(np.min(adj)); amax=float(np.max(adj))
       scale=max(abs(amin),abs(amax))
       if scale < 1e-13:
           break
       adj=adj/scale
       # If one component too large? clip exp arg
       adj=np.clip(adj,-5.0,5.0)
       lam = lam * np.exp(-0.5*adj)
       lam += 1e-12
       s=lam.sum()
       if s <= 0: lam=np.full(K,1.0/K)
       else: lam/=s
   return lam
```
- If M scaled, reg 1e-10 relative. Good. If M all zeros after scale? mmax handles. 
- Multiplicative sign: We want reduce grad high. factor exp(-0.5*adj). Good.

`local_search_active` with preallocated B. Need choose K values top-level:
```
def choose_K_values(n):
   if n <= 256:
      base=24; maxK=48
   elif n <= 512:
      base=32; maxK=64
   elif n <= 1024:
      base=48; maxK=96
   elif n <= 2048:
      base=80; maxK=160
   else:
      base=140; maxK=280
   maxK=min(maxK, 2*n-1)
   base=min(base,maxK)
   return base,maxK
```
For n=8192 base140 max280. M solve 280. OK. Could max 300.

def local_search_active(a, budget_s, seed=0):
   a=normalize_sum1(np.asarray(a,float))
   n=int(a.size)
   if n==0: return a, inf
   baseK,maxK=choose_K_values(n)
   c=fast_convolve(a)
   best_score=2*n*float(np.max(c))
   best=a.copy(); current=a.copy(); current_score=best_score
   if budget_s <= 2: return best,best_score
   t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget_s
   B=np.zeros((n,maxK), dtype=np.float64)
   Bw=np.zeros((n,maxK), dtype=np.float64)
   K=baseK
   eta=0.25; accept_rel=0.02; mom=np.zeros(n)
   stagnant=0; it=0; last_best_print=best_score
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.05:
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(current)) or float(current.sum())<1e-15:
           current=best.copy(); current_score=best_score; mom*=0; stagnant+=50
       if K>maxK: K=maxK
       if K<8: K=8
       idx=select_active(c,K)  # c from current last? Need if current reset, recompute c? At top of loop we use c from previous iteration. For first, computed. After accepting/rejecting, c=cand? We should update c if accept; if reject, c remains current. At restart, recompute. Let's recompute at start each loop to be safe (cost one conv). Already need for score. So c=fast_convolve(current).
```
Yes each loop start compute c.
```
       c=fast_convolve(current)
       M=float(np.max(c)); sc=2*n*M
       if not finite: reset
       if sc < best_score:
          best=current.copy(); best_score=sc; stagnant=0
          # maybe print if improvement >1e-5 and it%? 
       if (sc < best_score) ? best updated. For best tracking current maybe.
       idx=select_active(c,K)
       K_use=len(idx)
       build_active_basis(current,c,idx,B)
       sqrt_a=np.sqrt(current)
       np.multiply(B[:, :K_use], sqrt_a[:,None], out=Bw[:, :K_use])
       Mmat = Bw[:, :K_use].T @ Bw[:, :K_use]
       lam=solve_simplex_lambda(Mmat,K_use)
       g = B[:, :K_use] @ lam
       g -= float(np.dot(current,g))
       # momentum
       mom = 0.4*mom + 0.6*g
       g = mom
       g -= float(np.dot(current,g))
       gscale=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
       if gscale < 1e-13:
           stagnant += 1
           if stagnant > 100: break
           continue
       g /= gscale
       cand = current * np.exp(-eta * g)
       cand = normalize_sum1(cand)

               # stagnant not reset if not best
       else:
           eta=max(0.05, eta*0.75)
           mom *= 0.5
           stagnant += 1
       if stagnant > 300:
           strength = 0.05 + 0.0003*min(stagnant,400)
           strength = min(0.3, strength)
           cand2 = smooth_log_perturb(best, rng, strength)
           sc3 = fast_score(cand2)
           if np.isfinite(sc3) and sc3 <= best_score*1.08:
              current=cand2; current_score=sc3
           else:
              current=best.copy(); current_score=best_score
           eta=0.3; accept_rel=0.04; mom=zeros; stagnant=0
           # adapt K
           if K < maxK:
              K = min(maxK, int(K*1.5)+8)
           elif K > baseK:
              K = baseK
           # if still no progress after many restarts? count resets
       it += 1
   # After loop, maybe final exact? no. Return best
   return best, best_score
```

def run_level(n, budget, parent, seed, deadline):
    if budget <= 0: ...
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    starts = generate_starts(n, rng, parent)
    starts.sort(key=_pair_score)
    if not starts:
       arr=upsample_sequence(parent,n) if parent is not None else np.ones(n)
       arr=normalize_sum1(arr); return arr, fast_score(arr)
    best_seq, best_score = starts[0]
    if budget > 500: ks=[0.5,0.22,0.16,0.12]
    elif budget > 200: ks=[0.55,0.25,0.20]
    elif budget > 80: ks=[0.6,0.4]
    else: ks=[1.0]
    k=min(len(starts), len(ks))
    for i in range(k):
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now >= deadline: break
       time_this = min(ks[i]*budget, max(0, deadline - now - 1.0))
       if time_this <= 1.0: break
       seq,sc=local_search_active(starts[i][1], time_this, seed=seed*1000+i)
       if sc < best_score:
          best_seq=seq; best_score=sc
       print(f"      level {n} start {i}: {sc:.6f} (best {best_score:.6f})")
    return best_seq,best_score
```

`random_wave_start`: if n large, t array size n; okay.
```
def random_wave_start(n,rng):
   t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   z=np.zeros(n)
   nf=int(rng.integers(3,10))
   for m in range(1,nf+1):
       amp=float(rng.uniform(0.3,2.0)/(m**0.55))
       ph=float(rng.uniform(0.0,2*math.pi))
       z += amp*np.cos(2*math.pi*m*t+ph)
       ph2=float(rng.uniform(0.0,2*math.pi))
       z += amp*0.5*np.sin(2*math.pi*m*t+ph2)
   z += float(rng.uniform(-2.0,2.0))*(2.0*t-1.0)
   z -= float(np.max(z))
   a=np.exp(np.clip(z, -20.0, 20.0))
   return a
```
`block_start`:
```
def block_start(n,rng):
   nb=int(rng.integers(4, min(30,n//2)+1))
   # random lengths
   counts=rng.multinomial(n, np.ones(nb)/nb)
   while np.any(counts==0):
       counts[rng.integers(0,nb)] += 1
       # maybe not needed; multinomial can zero. Could just repeat.
   vals=rng.uniform(0.0,3.0,nb)
   # ensure sum positive
   vals[np.argmax(vals)] = max(vals.max(),1.0)
   a=np.repeat(vals, counts)
   return a
```

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
   t0=time.monotonic()
   budget=float(budget_s)
   if budget < 10: return sanitize_for_return(np.ones(1024))? But need know global? return [1.0]*1024
   search_deadline = t0 + max(10.0, budget - 60.0)
   global_best=None; global_score=inf
   pool=[]
   init=get_initial_sequence()
   if init is not None:
       s=fast_score(init)
       global_best=init; global_score=s; add_pool(pool, init, s)
       print("initial", global_score)
   # Determine plan
   remaining = search_deadline - t0
   if budget >= 1800:
      plan=[(512,40),(1024,120),(2048,300),(4096,900),(8192,500)]
   elif budget >= 900:
      plan=[(512,40),(1024,150),(2048,400),(4096,1200)]
   elif budget >= 400:
      plan=[(512,60),(1024,200),(2048,400)]
   elif budget >= 200:
      plan=[(512,80),(1024,200)]
   else:
      plan=[(512,60)]
   # cap plan to search_deadline; skip if too small
   for idx,(n,b) in enumerate(plan):
       now=time.monotonic()

       level_budget = min(b, search_deadline - now)
       if level_budget <= 20: continue
       # ensure enough for later? If this is not last and level_budget > search_deadline-now-60? cap?

       except Exception:
           seq,sc=None,inf

           global_best=seq; global_score=sc
       # Add pool with seq even if not better? Could add top; add_pool sorted by score, keep best.
       if seq is not None:

   final_n = global_best.size if global_best is not None else 1024

       add_pool(pool, global_best, global_score)
   # exact validation
   pool.sort(key=_pair_score)
   exact_best_seq=global_best
   exact_best_score=global_score

           break

   if exact_best_seq is None or not np.isfinite(exact_best_score):
       exact_best_seq=global_best
   return sanitize_for_return(exact_best_seq)

Let's write `smooth_log_perturb` carefully:
```
def smooth_log_perturb(a, rng, strength):
    n=int(a.size)
    out=np.asarray(a,float).copy()
    out=np.maximum(out,1e-12)
    if strength > 0 and n > 2:
        t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
        z=np.zeros(n,float)
        for m in range(1,6):
            amp=strength/(0.5+0.7*m)
            ph=float(rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*math.pi))
            z += amp*np.sin(2.0*math.pi*m*t + ph)
            ph2=float(rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*math.pi))
            z += amp*0.6*np.cos(2.0*math.pi*m*t + ph2)
        out *= np.exp(z)
        if rng.random() < 0.4 and n > 8:
            width=max(2, int(n/100))
            start=int(rng.integers(0, n-width+1))
            seg=int(rng.normal(0.0, strength, width))
            out[start:start+width] *= np.exp(seg)
    return normalize_sum1(out)
```
`n-width+1` if width>=n? width=n/100 so <n. Good.

Let's draft `add_pool`:
```
def add_pool(pool, seq, score, max_pool=16):
    try:
       sc=float(score)
    except:
       return
    if seq is None or not np.isfinite(sc):
       return
    arr=np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    pool.append((sc, arr))
    pool.sort(key=_pair_score)
    if len(pool)>max_pool:
       del pool[-1]
```

def arcsine_start(n,rng):
   t=np.linspace(1e-4,1-1e-4,n)
   a=1.0/np.sqrt(t*(1.0-t))
   # random skew
   a *= np.exp(float(rng.uniform(-2.0,2.0))*(2.0*t-1.0))
   a *= np.exp(float(rng.uniform(-1.0,1.0))*t**2)
   return a
```

Let's design `block_start` safely:
```
def block_start(n,rng):
   if n < 4: return np.ones(n)
   max_blocks = max(4, min(30, n//4))
   nb = int(rng.integers(4, max_blocks+1))
   counts = rng.multinomial(n, np.full(nb, 1.0/nb))
   # ensure positive
   for i in range(nb):
       if counts[i] == 0:
           counts[i] = 1
           # subtract from a positive block
           pos = np.nonzero(counts>1)[0]
           if len(pos): counts[pos[0]] -=1
   vals = rng.uniform(0.0,3.0,nb)
   if vals.sum() <= 1e-12: vals.fill(1.0)
   a = np.repeat(vals, counts)
   # counts sum may change; if not n, resize? We can fix by if size != n: upsample? Better not modify counts to keep sum. Instead if zeros, ignore? np.repeat with zeros okay. But values length nb, if count zero, no issue. We can skip positive fix. Uniform may all zero. If all zero, vals max. Let's not modify counts; zeros okay. vals sum positive by setting max.
   vals[int(np.argmax(vals))] = max(float(vals.max()),1.0)
   a=np.repeat(vals, counts)
   if a.size != n: # due to fix? no
       a=upsample_sequence(a,n)
   return a
```

`normalize_sum1`: If n==0 returns [1]. But initial functions expect n>0. For zero, okay.
```
def normalize_sum1(a):
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=arr.size
    if n==0:
       return np.ones((1,), dtype=np.float64)
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.maximum(arr,0.0)
    arr=np.maximum(arr,1e-12)
    s=float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0.0:
       return np.full(n,1.0/float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    arr=arr/s
    return arr
```
If n huge floor sum maybe >? okay.

`upsample_sequence`:
```
def upsample_sequence(seq,n):
   n=int(n)
   if n<=0: return np.ones((1,),float)
   arr=np.asarray(seq,float)
   if arr.size==0: return np.ones(n,float)
   if arr.size==n: return normalize_sum1(arr)
   if arr.size==1: return np.full(n,1.0/float(n)) # normalize of constant
   # If arr values maybe NaN
   arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
   arr=np.maximum(arr,0.0)
   s=arr.sum()
   if s<=0: return np.full(n,1.0/n)
   x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,arr.size)
   y=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   out=np.interp(y,x,arr)
   return normalize_sum1(out)
```
If downsample from large to small, interpolation averages to some extent. Good.

c=np.ascontiguousarray(c,dtype? irfft returns float)
bad=~np.isfinite(c); if bad: c[bad]=0
np.maximum(c,0,out=c)
return c
```

`run_level`: Use `max_starts = 130 if n >= 2048 else 80`. If budget >500 maybe 160. 
```
def run_level(n, budget, parent, seed, deadline):
    n=int(n); budget=float(max(0,budget))
    if budget <= 5:
       if parent is not None: arr=upsample_sequence(parent,n)
       else: arr=np.ones(n)
       arr=normalize_sum1(arr); return arr, fast_score(arr)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    max_starts = 70
    if budget > 200: max_starts=110
    if budget > 600: max_starts=150
    starts=generate_starts(n,rng,parent,max_starts)
    if not starts:
       arr=upsample_sequence(parent,n) if parent is not None else np.ones(n)
       arr=normalize_sum1(arr); return arr, fast_score(arr)
    best_seq, best_score = starts[0][1], starts[0][0]
    if budget > 1200: ks=[0.45,0.25,0.18,0.12]
    elif budget > 600: ks=[0.5,0.25,0.25]
    elif budget > 250: ks=[0.55,0.45]
    else: ks=[1.0]
    for i in range(min(len(starts), len(ks))):
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now >= deadline: break
       avail=deadline-now
       time_this=min(ks[i]*budget, avail-1.0)
       if time_this <= 1.0: break
       try:
          seq,sc=local_search_active(starts[i][1], time_this, seed=int(seed*1000+i)+1)
       except Exception:
          seq=None; sc=float('inf')
       if seq is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_score:
          best_seq=seq.copy(); best_score=sc
       print(f"        level {n} start {i}: {sc:.6f}")
    return best_seq,best_score
```
If budget small but >5, local may not run; returns top start. Good.

Potential issue: `print` inside run_level for each start; okay.

`local_search_active`: Need if `budget_s` <=2: return start. If `maxK` > 2*n-1? choose cap. Prealloc B. If n large memory; okay. 
Loop details with variables:
```
def local_search_active(a, budget_s, seed=12345):
    a=normalize_sum1(a)
    n=int(a.size)
    c=fast_convolve(a)
    M=float(np.max(c)); best_score=2.0*float(n)*M
    best=a.copy(); current=a.copy(); current_score=best_score
    if budget_s <= 2.0:
       return best,best_score
    baseK,maxK=choose_K_values(n)
    if maxK <1: maxK=1
    B=np.zeros((maxK,n),float)
    Bw=np.zeros((maxK,n),float)
    K=baseK
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget_s
    eta=0.5; accept_rel=0.02; mom=np.zeros(n)
    stagnant=0; restarts=0; it=0; last_print=best_score
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    K_min=max(1, baseK//2)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.05:
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(current)) or float(current.sum())<1e-15:
            current=best.copy(); current_score=best_score; mom.fill(0); stagnant+=20
        c=fast_convolve(current)
        M=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else float('inf')
        sc=2.0*float(n)*M
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            current=best.copy(); c=fast_convolve(current); current_score=best_score; continue
        if sc < best_score:
            best=current.copy(); best_score=sc; stagnant=0; restarts=0
            if best_score < last_print - 5e-5:
                print(f"          local n={n} it={it} best={best_score:.6f}")
                last_print=best_score
        idx=select_active(c,K)
        K_use=int(idx.size)
        if K_use <=0:
            break
        if K_use > maxK:
            continue # shouldn't
        build_active_basis_row(current,c,idx,B)
        sqrt_a=np.sqrt(current)
        np.multiply(B[:K_use,:], sqrt_a, out=Bw[:K_use,:])
        Mmat=Bw[:K_use,:].dot(Bw[:K_use,:].T)
        # ensure finite
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(Mmat)):
            lam=np.full(K_use,1.0/K_use)
        else:
            lam=solve_simplex_lambda(Mmat,K_use)
        if lam is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(lam)):
            lam=np.full(K_use,1.0/K_use)
        g=B[:K_use,:].T.dot(lam)
        g=g.astype(np.float64, copy=True)? dot returns float.
        g -= float(np.dot(current,g))
        mom = 0.35*mom + 0.65*g
        g=mom
        g -= float(np.dot(current,g))
        g_abs=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if not np.isfinite(g_abs) or g_abs < 1e-14:
            stagnant += 1
            if stagnant > 100:
                break
            continue
        step_strength = min(1.0, 2.0*float(n)*g_abs)
        if step_strength < 1e-4:
            stagnant += 1
            if stagnant > 100: break
            continue
        g /= g_abs
        # clip g to avoid huge exp? g max1, eta up to maybe 1.2.
        cand=current * np.exp(-eta*step_strength*g)
        cand=normalize_sum1(cand)
        c2=fast_convolve(cand)
        M2=float(np.max(c2)) if c2.size else inf
        sc2=2*n*M2
        if not finite:
            eta=max(0.1, eta*0.75); mom*=0.5; stagnant+=1; continue
        if sc2 <= current_score*(1.0+accept_rel):
            current=cand; current_score=sc2; c=c2
            mom *= 0.6
            if sc2 < best_score:
                best=current.copy(); best_score=sc2; stagnant=0; restarts=0
                eta=min(1.2, eta*1.08)
                accept_rel=max(0.001, accept_rel*0.99)
                if best_score < last_print - 5e-5: print...
            else:
                eta=max(0.08, eta*0.94)
                stagnant += 1
        else:
            eta=max(0.08, eta*0.78)
            mom *= 0.4
            stagnant +=1
        if stagnant > 300:
            strength=0.05 + 0.0002*min(500, stagnant)
            strength=min(0.25, strength)
            cand2=smooth_log_perturb(best,rng,strength)
            sc3=fast_score(cand2)
            if np.isfinite(sc3) and sc3 <= best_score*1.10:
                current=cand2; current_score=sc3; c=fast_convolve(cand2)
            else:
                current=best.copy(); current_score=best_score; c=fast_convolve(current)
            eta=0.5; accept_rel=0.03; mom.fill(0); stagnant=0; restarts +=1
            if K < maxK:
                K = min(maxK, int(K*1.5)+8)
            elif K > baseK:
                K = baseK
            if restarts > 14:
                break
        it += 1
    return best,best_score
```

    n=int(n)
    if n <= 256: base=24; maxK=48
    elif n <=512: base=32; maxK=64

    L=2*n-1

    maxK=max(maxK,base)

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing

    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

The implementation uses:
- sum-normalized, non-negative sequences (objective is scale invariant),
- FFT convolution for fast objective/geometry evaluation,
- multi-resolution refinement from shorter sequences to longer ones,
- a custom local optimizer based on active-peak weighting:
  for a chosen active peak set, solve a small nonnegative quadratic
  program for weights lambda, then take a multiplicative projected-gradient
  step in the simplex of valid sequences,
- diverse starts including the provided previous best, random waves,
  blocks, Gaussian mixtures, arcsine-like edge-weighted shapes, and
  skewed versions of current best sequences.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math


def _pair_score(pair):
    return float(pair[0])


def normalize_sum1(a):
    """Non-negative, finite, sum normalized to 1."""
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = arr.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones((1,), dtype=np.float64)

    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    # Tiny absolute floor to keep all coordinates addressable.
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 1e-12)

    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)

    arr = arr / s
    return arr


def upsample_sequence(seq, n):
    """Interpolate or downsample a sequence to length n, then normalize sum to 1."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones((1,), dtype=np.float64)

    arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if arr.size == n:
        return normalize_sum1(arr)

    if arr.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)

    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)

    xold = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
    xnew = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.interp(xnew, xold, arr)
    return normalize_sum1(out)


def next_power_two(x):
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(x) - 1).bit_length()


def fast_convolve_fixed(a, N, L):
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:L]
    c = np.ascontiguousarray(c)
    c[~np.isfinite(c)] = 0.0
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def fast_convolve(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_power_two(L)
    return fast_convolve_fixed(a, N, L)


def fast_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = fast_convolve(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * M)


def get_initial_sequence():
    """Use previous best if provided as height_sequence_1."""
    hs = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if hs is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(hs, dtype=np.float64)
        if arr.size == 0:
            return None
        arr = normalize_sum1(arr)
        if arr.size > 10000:
            arr = upsample_sequence(arr, 4096)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
            return None
        return arr
    except Exception:
        return None


def add_start(starts, seq, n):
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
        if arr.size != n:
            arr = upsample_sequence(arr, n)
        else:
            arr = normalize_sum1(arr)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
            return
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if s < 1e-12:
            arr = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            arr = arr / s

        sc = fast_score(arr)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            starts.append((sc, arr.copy()))
    except Exception:
        pass


def block_start(n, rng):
    if n < 4:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    max_blocks = max(4, min(30, n // 4))
    nb = int(rng.integers(4, max_blocks + 1))
    counts = rng.multinomial(n, np.full(nb, 1.0 / float(nb)))
    vals = rng.uniform(0.0, 3.0, nb)
    imax = int(np.argmax(vals))
    vals[imax] = max(float(vals[imax]), 1.0)
    a = np.repeat(vals, counts)
    if a.size != n:
        a = upsample_sequence(a, n)
    return a


def two_block_start(n, rng):
    if n < 4:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    frac = float(rng.uniform(0.15, 0.85))
    m = int(max(1, min(n - 1, frac * n)))
    ratio = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 8.0))
    high = max(ratio, 1.0 / ratio)
    low = 1.0 / high if high != 1.0 else 1.0

    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if rng.random() < 0.5:
        a[:m] = high
        a[m:] = low
    else:
        a[:m] = low
        a[m:] = high
    return a


def cluster_start(n, rng):
    if n < 8:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.full(n, 0.01, dtype=np.float64)
    k = int(rng.integers(2, 5))
    for _ in range(k):
        pos = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.95))
        w = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.13))
        amp = float(rng.expovariate(1.0) + 0.25)
        a += amp * np.exp(-0.5 * ((t - pos) / w) ** 2)
    return a


def random_wave_start(n, rng):
    if n < 4:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    nf = int(rng.integers(3, 10))
    nf = max(1, min(nf, n // 4 + 1))
    for m in range(1, nf + 1):
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 2.0) / (m ** 0.55))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        ph2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        z += amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * m * t + ph)
        z += 0.5 * amp * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * m * t + ph2)
    z += float(rng.uniform(-2.0, 2.0)) * (2.0 * t - 1.0)
    z -= float(np.max(z))
    z = np.clip(z, -30.0, 30.0)
    return np.exp(z)


def ushape_start(n, rng):
    if n < 4:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    p = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 2.0))
    q = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 2.0))
    amp = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 6.0))
    shift = float(rng.uniform(-2.5, 2.5))
    a = 1.0 + amp * (np.power(t, p) + np.power(1.0 - t, q)) + shift * (2.0 * t - 1.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-6)
    return a


def arcsine_start(n, rng):
    if n < 4:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.linspace(1e-5, 1.0 - 1e-5, n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = 1.0 / np.sqrt(t * (1.0 - t))
    a *= np.exp(float(rng.uniform(-2.5, 2.5)) * (2.0 * t - 1.0))
    a *= np.exp(float(rng.uniform(-1.5, 1.5)) * t)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-6)
    return a


def generate_starts(n, rng, parent, max_starts=80):
    starts = []
    n = int(n)

    if parent is not None and np.asarray(parent).size > 0:
        p_up = upsample_sequence(parent, n)
        add_start(starts, p_up, n)
        add_start(starts, p_up[::-1], n)

        if n > 1:
            t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
            for alpha in (-2.5, -1.2, 0.6, 1.5, 2.5):
                add_start(starts, p_up * np.exp(alpha * (t - 0.5)), n)

        for s in (0.08, 0.18, 0.30):
            add_start(starts, smooth_log_perturb(p_up, rng, s), n)

    hs = get_initial_sequence()
    if hs is not None:
        h_up = upsample_sequence(hs, n)
        add_start(starts, h_up, n)
        add_start(starts, h_up[::-1], n)
        if n > 1:
            t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
            for alpha in (-2.0, 1.0):
                add_start(starts, h_up * np.exp(alpha * (t - 0.5)), n)

    add_start(starts, np.ones(n), n)

    count = 0
    while len(starts) < max_starts and count < 2 * max_starts:
        count += 1
        r = float(rng.random())
        if r < 0.15:
            a = arcsine_start(n, rng)
        elif r < 0.30:
            a = block_start(n, rng)
        elif r < 0.50:
            a = two_block_start(n, rng)
        elif r < 0.70:
            a = cluster_start(n, rng)
        elif r < 0.85:
            a = random_wave_start(n, rng)
        else:
            a = ushape_start(n, rng)
        add_start(starts, a, n)

    starts.sort(key=_pair_score)
    return starts[:max_starts]


def smooth_log_perturb(a, rng, strength):
    """Multiplicative smooth low-frequency perturbation, useful for restarts."""
    n = int(np.asarray(a).size)
    out = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    out = np.maximum(out, 1e-12)

    if strength > 0.0 and n > 2:
        t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
        z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for m in range(1, 6):
            amp = strength / (0.5 + 0.7 * m)
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
            ph2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
            z += amp * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * m * t + ph)
            z += 0.6 * amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * m * t + ph2)
        out *= np.exp(z)

        if n > 16 and rng.random() < 0.4:
            width = max(2, int(n // 100))
            width = min(width, n - 1)
            start = int(rng.integers(0, n - width + 1))
            vals = rng.normal(0.0, max(1e-8, strength), width)
            out[start:start + width] *= np.exp(vals)

    return normalize_sum1(out)


def choose_K_values(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 256:
        base = 24
        maxK = 48
    elif n <= 512:
        base = 32
        maxK = 64
    elif n <= 1024:
        base = 48
        maxK = 96
    elif n <= 2048:
        base = 80
        maxK = 160
    else:
        base = 140
        maxK = 280

    L = 2 * n - 1
    maxK = min(maxK, L)
    base = min(base, maxK)
    maxK = max(maxK, base, 1)
    base = max(base, 1)
    return base, maxK


def select_active(c, K):
    L = c.size
    K = int(K)
    if K <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=int)
    if K >= L:
        return np.arange(L, dtype=int)
    idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
    return idx.astype(int, copy=False)


def build_active_basis_row(a, c, idx, B):
    """
    For each active lag k, store row b_i = a_{k-i} - c[k].
    Rows are centered so that weighted mean with weight a is (near) zero.
    """
    n = a.size
    K = idx.size
    for j in range(K):
        k = int(idx[j])
        row = B[j]
        row.fill(0.0)

        start = k - (n - 1)
        if start < 0:
            start = 0
        stop = k + 1
        if stop > n:
            stop = n

        if stop > start:
            src_start = k - (stop - 1)
            src_end = k - start + 1
            row[start:stop] = a[src_start:src_end][::-1]

        row -= float(c[k])

    return K


def project_simplex(v):
    """Euclidean projection onto {x >= 0, sum(x)=1}."""
    K = len(v)
    if K == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if K == 1:
        return np.ones((1,), dtype=np.float64)

    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    rho = np.nonzero(u * (np.arange(1, K + 1)) - (cssv - 1.0) > 0)[0]
    if len(rho) == 0:
        return np.full(K, 1.0 / float(K), dtype=np.float64)

    rho = int(rho[-1])
    theta = (cssv[rho] - 1.0) / float(rho + 1)
    z = v - theta
    z = np.maximum(z, 0.0)
    s = float(z.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(K, 1.0 / float(K), dtype=np.float64)
    return z / s


def solve_simplex_lambda(M, K):
    """
    Approximate solution of

        min lambda^T M lambda
        s.t. lambda >= 0, sum(lambda)=1.

    M is PSD (possibly nearly singular).  First project the unconstrained
    equality minimizer, then refine with a few multiplicative gradient steps.
    """
    if K <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if K == 1:
        return np.ones((1,), dtype=np.float64)

    M = np.asarray(M, dtype=np.float64)
    M = 0.5 * (M + M.T)

    mmax = float(np.max(np.abs(M))) if M.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(mmax) or mmax < 1e-300:
        return np.full(K, 1.0 / float(K), dtype=np.float64)

    M = M / mmax
    diag = np.diag_indices(K)
    M[diag] += 1e-10

    lam = None
    try:
        x = np.linalg.solve(M, np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64))
        if np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            lam = project_simplex(x)
    except Exception:
        lam = None

    if lam is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(lam)):
        lam = np.full(K, 1.0 / float(K), dtype=np.float64)

    for _ in range(8):
        grad = M.dot(lam)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
            break

        center = float(np.dot(lam, grad))
        adj = grad - center
        amin = float(np.min(adj))
        amax = float(np.max(adj))
        scale = max(abs(amin), abs(amax))
        if scale < 1e-13:
            break

        adj = adj / scale
        adj = np.clip(adj, -5.0, 5.0)
        lam = lam * np.exp(-0.5 * adj)
        lam += 1e-12
        s = float(lam.sum())
        if s <= 0.0:
            lam = np.full(K, 1.0 / float(K), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            lam = lam / s

    return lam


def local_search_active(a, budget_s, seed=12345):
    """
    Local optimizer in the nonnegative simplex.

    For the current sequence, select active convolution peaks.  For those peaks,
    solve a small SDP-like weighting problem with lambda >= 0, sum(lambda)=1,
    and minimize the variance of the corresponding subgradient.  Then perform
    a multiplicative projected-gradient step in log coordinates.
    """
    a = normalize_sum1(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        a = normalize_sum1(np.ones(1024))
        n = a.size

    c = fast_convolve(a)
    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else float("inf")
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * M)
    best = a.copy()
    current = a.copy()
    current_score = best_score

    if budget_s <= 2.0 or not np.isfinite(best_score):
        return best, best_score

    baseK, maxK = choose_K_values(n)
    B = np.zeros((maxK, n), dtype=np.float64)
    Bw = np.zeros((maxK, n), dtype=np.float64)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + float(max(0.0, budget_s))

    K = baseK
    K_min = max(1, baseK // 2)
    eta = 0.5
    accept_rel = 0.02
    mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    stagnant = 0
    restarts = 0
    it = 0
    last_print_score = best_score
    need_score = True

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.05:
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(current)) or float(current.sum()) < 1e-15:
            current = best.copy()
            current_score = best_score
            mom.fill(0.0)
            need_score = True
            stagnant += 20

        if need_score:
            c = fast_convolve(current)
            M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else float("inf")
            sc = float(2.0 * n * M)
            if not np.isfinite(sc):
                current = best.copy()
                current_score = best_score
                need_score = True
                continue
            current_score = sc

            if sc < best_score:
                best = current.copy()
                best_score = sc
                stagnant = 0
                restarts = 0
                if best_score < last_print_score - 5e-5:
                    print(f"          local n={n} it={it} best={best_score:.6f}")
                    last_print_score = best_score

            need_score = False

        idx = select_active(c, K)
        K_use = int(idx.size)
        if K_use <= 0:
            break

        if K_use > maxK:
            K_use = maxK
            idx = idx[:K_use]

        build_active_basis_row(current, c, idx, B)

        sqrt_a = np.sqrt(current)
        np.multiply(B[:K_use, :], sqrt_a, out=Bw[:K_use, :])
        Mmat = Bw[:K_use, :].dot(Bw[:K_use, :].T)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(Mmat)):
            lam = np.full(K_use, 1.0 / float(K_use), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            lam = solve_simplex_lambda(Mmat, K_use)

        if lam is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(lam)):
            lam = np.full(K_use, 1.0 / float(K_use), dtype=np.float64)

        g = B[:K_use, :].T.dot(lam)
        g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        g -= float(np.dot(current, g))

        mom = 0.35 * mom + 0.65 * g
        g = mom
        g -= float(np.dot(current, g))

        g_abs = float(np.max(np.abs(g))) if g.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(g_abs) or g_abs < 1e-14:
            stagnant += 1
            if stagnant > 120:
                break
            continue

        step_strength = min(1.0, 2.0 * float(n) * g_abs)
        if step_strength < 1e-5:
            stagnant += 1
            if stagnant > 120:
                break
            continue

        g /= g_abs
        cand = current * np.exp(-eta * step_strength * g)
        cand = normalize_sum1(cand)

        c2 = fast_convolve(cand)
        M2 = float(np.max(c2)) if c2.size else float("inf")
        sc2 = float(2.0 * n * M2)

        if not np.isfinite(sc2):
            eta = max(0.08, eta * 0.75)
            mom *= 0.5
            stagnant += 1
            continue

        if sc2 <= current_score * (1.0 + accept_rel):
            current = cand
            current_score = sc2
            c = c2
            need_score = False
            mom *= 0.6

            if sc2 < best_score:
                best = current.copy()
                best_score = sc2
                stagnant = 0
                restarts = 0
                eta = min(1.2, eta * 1.08)
                accept_rel = max(0.001, accept_rel * 0.99)
                if best_score < last_print_score - 5e-5:
                    print(f"          local n={n} it={it} best={best_score:.6f}")
                    last_print_score = best_score
            else:
                eta = max(0.08, eta * 0.94)
                stagnant += 1
        else:
            eta = max(0.08, eta * 0.78)
            mom *= 0.4
            stagnant += 1

        if stagnant > 260:
            strength = 0.05 + 0.0002 * min(500, stagnant)
            strength = min(0.25, strength)
            cand2 = smooth_log_perturb(best, rng, strength)
            sc3 = fast_score(cand2)

            if np.isfinite(sc3) and sc3 <= best_score * 1.10:
                current = cand2
                current_score = sc3
            else:
                current = best.copy()
                current_score = best_score

            c = fast_convolve(current)
            need_score = False
            eta = 0.5
            accept_rel = 0.03
            mom.fill(0.0)
            stagnant = 0
            restarts += 1

            if K < maxK:
                K = min(maxK, int(K * 1.5) + 8)
            elif K > baseK:
                K = K_min if K_min < K else baseK

            if restarts > 14:
                break

        it += 1

    return best, best_score


def run_level(n, budget, parent, seed, deadline):
    """Generate starts for a length and refine a few of the best ones."""
    n = int(n)
    budget = float(max(0.0, budget))

    if budget <= 20.0:
        if parent is not None:
            arr = upsample_sequence(parent, n)
        else:
            arr = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        arr = normalize_sum1(arr)
        return arr, fast_score(arr)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    if budget > 600.0:
        max_starts = 150
    elif budget > 250.0:
        max_starts = 110
    else:
        max_starts = 70

    starts = generate_starts(n, rng, parent, max_starts=max_starts)

    if not starts:
        arr = upsample_sequence(parent, n) if parent is not None else np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        arr = normalize_sum1(arr)
        return arr, fast_score(arr)

    best_seq, best_score = starts[0][1], starts[0][0]

    if budget > 1200.0:
        frac = [0.45, 0.25, 0.18, 0.12]
    elif budget > 600.0:
        frac = [0.50, 0.25, 0.25]
    elif budget > 250.0:
        frac = [0.55, 0.45]
    else:
        frac = [1.0]

    k = min(len(starts), len(frac))
    for i in range(k):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline:
            break

        avail = float(deadline - now)
        if avail <= 2.0:
            break

        time_this = min(frac[i] * budget, avail - 1.0)
        if time_this <= 1.0:
            break

        try:
            seq, sc = local_search_active(starts[i][1], time_this, seed=int(seed * 1000 + i + 1))
        except Exception:
            seq = None
            sc = float("inf")

        if seq is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_seq = seq.copy()
            best_score = sc

        print(f"        level n={n} start={i} score={sc:.6f} best={best_score:.6f}")

    return best_seq, best_score


def add_pool(pool, seq, score, max_pool=16):
    try:
        sc = float(score)
    except Exception:
        return
    if seq is None or not np.isfinite(sc):
        return
    arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    pool.append((sc, arr))
    pool.sort(key=_pair_score)
    if len(pool) > max_pool:
        del pool[-1]


def sanitize_for_return(seq):
    """Return a safe Python list of non-negative finite floats."""
    arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)

    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
    elif arr.size > 10000:
        arr = upsample_sequence(arr, 8192)

    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)

    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return [1.0] * 1024

    arr = arr / s
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)

    if float(arr.sum()) < 0.01:
        return [1.0] * 1024

    return [float(x) for x in arr]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Main entry point.

    Coarse-to-fine search over sequence length, with active-set quadratic
    weighted projected gradient refinement.  Returns the best exact-validated
    sequence found, or the best fast-scored one if exact validation fails.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(max(0.0, budget_s))

    init = get_initial_sequence()

    if budget < 5.0:
        if init is not None:
            return sanitize_for_return(init)
        return [1.0] * 1024

    reserve = max(30.0, min(120.0, 0.05 * max(1.0, budget)))
    total_search = max(30.0, budget - reserve)
    search_deadline = t0 + total_search
    exact_deadline = t0 + max(60.0, budget - 5.0)

    global_best = None
    global_score = float("inf")
    pool = []

    if init is not None:
        sc = fast_score(init)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            global_best = init.copy()
            global_score = sc
            add_pool(pool, init, sc)
            print(f"initial best: n={int(init.size)} score={sc:.6f}")

    if budget >= 1800.0:
        plan = [
            (512, 0.020),
            (1024, 0.050),
            (2048, 0.120),
            (4096, 0.380),
            (8192, 0.330),
        ]
    elif budget >= 900.0:
        plan = [
            (512, 0.040),
            (1024, 0.080),
            (2048, 0.200),
            (4096, 0.680),
        ]
    elif budget >= 400.0:
        plan = [
            (512, 0.100),
            (1024, 0.250),
            (2048, 0.650),
        ]
    elif budget >= 200.0:
        plan = [
            (512, 0.300),
            (1024, 0.700),
        ]
    else:
        plan = [
            (512, 1.0),
        ]

    for level_idx, (n, fr) in enumerate(plan):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= search_deadline - 20.0:
            break

        level_budget = 0.90 * float(fr) * total_search
        if level_budget <= 20.0:
            continue

        try:
            seq, sc = run_level(
                n,
                level_budget,
                global_best,
                int(seed + 10000 + 1000 * level_idx),
                search_deadline
            )
        except Exception:
            seq = None
            sc = float("inf")

        if seq is not None:
            add_pool(pool, seq, sc)

        if seq is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < global_score:
            global_best = seq.copy()
            global_score = sc

        print(
            f"level n={n} done: level_best={sc:.6f}, "
            f"global_best={global_score:.6f}, used={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s"
        )

    # If a large final length was not used and enough time remains, try it.
    if (
        global_best is not None
        and time.monotonic() < search_deadline - 180.0
        and int(global_best.size) < 8192
    ):
        target_n = 8192
        extra = min(700.0, 0.80 * float(search_deadline - time.monotonic()))
        if extra > 60.0:
            try:
                seq, sc = run_level(
                    target_n,
                    extra,
                    global_best,
                    int(seed + 987654),
                    search_deadline
                )
            except Exception:
                seq = None
                sc = float("inf")

            if seq is not None:
                add_pool(pool, seq, sc)
            if seq is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < global_score:
                global_best = seq.copy()
                global_score = sc
            print(f"extra large n={target_n}: score={sc:.6f}, global={global_score:.6f}")

    # Final polish at the current best length.
    if global_best is not None and time.monotonic() < search_deadline - 15.0:
        polish_budget = min(300.0, float(search_deadline - time.monotonic()))
        if polish_budget > 20.0:
            try:
                seq, sc = local_search_active(
                    global_best,
                    polish_budget,
                    seed=int(seed + 54321)
                )
            except Exception:
                seq = None
                sc = float("inf")

            if seq is not None:
                add_pool(pool, seq, sc)
            if seq is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < global_score:
                global_best = seq.copy()
                global_score = sc
            print(f"final polish: score={sc:.6f}, global={global_score:.6f}")

    if global_best is None:
        global_best = normalize_sum1(np.ones(1024))
        global_score = fast_score(global_best)
        add_pool(pool, global_best, global_score)

    # Exact validation of a few best candidates.
    pool.sort(key=_pair_score)
    exact_best_seq = global_best
    exact_best_score = float(global_score) if np.isfinite(global_score) else float("inf")

    tried = 0
    large_tried = 0
    for i, (sc, seq) in enumerate(pool[:8]):
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            continue
        if tried >= 7:
            break
        n_seq = int(np.asarray(seq).size)
        large = n_seq > 6000
        if large and large_tried >= 2:
            continue

        slack = 40.0 if large else 15.0
        if time.monotonic() > exact_deadline - slack:
            break

        try:
            ex = float(evaluate_sequence(seq.tolist()))
        except Exception:
            ex = float("inf")

        if np.isfinite(ex) and ex < exact_best_score:
            exact_best_score = ex
            exact_best_seq = seq
            print(f"exact candidate {i}: n={n_seq} score={ex:.6f}")

        tried += 1
        if large:
            large_tried += 1

    if exact_best_seq is None or not np.isfinite(exact_best_score):
        exact_best_seq = global_best

    print(f"final returned fast/exact best: {exact_best_score:.6f}")
    return sanitize_for_return(exact_best_seq)
```
